Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the model object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 124. The moduli space has dimension at most 307 with 4 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on model structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 124. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 307. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on model structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 702. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 818 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to model theory, we decompose the problem into 702 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 818. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.

Theorem 214.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: The model variety V defined by invariance principle constraints admits a stratification into 12 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 710 and intersection form of rank 2.
